# Design lock register (Matt, 2026-10-15)

Rule: design each thing fully, get Matt's approval, LOCK it here, then build exactly that. A locked item is only reopened if (a) it no longer fits how the game has evolved (a later locked decision conflicts with it), or (b) Matt says so. Bugs are fixed freely (a bug is behaviour that contradicts the locked design). Changing the design needs Matt's explicit reopen, with a one-line reason recorded here.

## Reopened (with reason)
- 2026-10-16: BIBLE.md section 'Defeat' line 'The hero's lantern goes out' REOPENED by Matt with the RESULTS SCREEN lock: the defeat lantern DIMS, not out (small flame still lit in a dim amber pool). Reason: a snuffed lantern reads as punishing and conflicts with the Hollowlight tone (bittersweet, hopeful) and with the fast-retry Again beat. BIBLE.md updated to 'dims, not out' in U6.
- 2026-10-15: Abilities row: Rally the Pack now also calls Ravens (Matt approved: Ravens become a real summon). Reason: the weapons design makes Ravens summons, so the Wren command should include them.

- 2026-10-15 A1b wolf shrink (12% smaller, fainter) KEPT by Matt.

- 2026-10-15 A2 Elias v2 in game: Matt reviewed, 'looks fine'. Polish parked (wheel-planting anim, glad face, flame count by rank, extra views). Heroes stage of identity build DONE; next = world sheet #2.

- 2026-10-16 WORLD SHEET #2 (docs/mockups/world-sheet/) LOCKED by Matt: ground/props = A Meadow Glade + B Deep Forest Floor by zone (A: opening/road/wells; B: grove/thicket/graves/edge); night light lifted ~14% (ARC retune in W6); HUD stays frost-only (no life-map tint). Build chunks W0-W8 per world-sheet README, each needs Matt's go-ahead; W0 = life map + blend spike first.
- 2026-10-16 W0 SPIKE BUILT on dev 2026-10-16 (awaiting Matt): life map + ground blend proven in the real game. src/world/lifemap.js (pure JS 256x256, visual only, sim untouched), src/gfx/groundblend.js (Phaser 3.90 Shader object: Hollow tile vs restored tile by life texture, uploaded ~10 Hz, graceful fallback), Low tier = flat Hollow tile + warm pool sprites, ?life=1 debug view, ?ground=flat forces the fallback, tests/lifemap.test.mjs, scripts/w0.mjs, docs/screenshots/w0/. VIABLE on desktop GPU (60 fps, +~0.5 ms CPU); NOT yet proven on a real phone GPU (needs Matt's phone check). Placeholder tiles; W1 is next only on Matt's go-ahead.

- 2026-10-16 W0 spike reviewed by Matt: approach liked (life map + blend + Low fallback kept). FEEDBACK: world still needs to feel more ALIVE. Applies to W1-W5 scope: add ambient life (grass/flower sway, drifting motes/fireflies, small critters/birds, wind in props, creature-reactive foliage) and check it against Low-tier perf. Real-phone shader test (?debug=1) still outstanding.

- 2026-10-16 AL-2 RESTORE-BLOOM BUILT on dev (Matt's go; awaiting his look): when ground BECOMES restored (a prop's life sample was seen below ENTER while in view, then rises) its flower/tuft appears as a bud and pops open when life crosses 0.5 (flowers: bud 0.4 -> ease-out-back ~0.5 s, ~6% overshoot, 0.5 rad rotate-in, + 2 tiny cream petal sparkles; tufts: shorter sprout 0.38 s, no sparkle). Props that scroll in already restored are simply open (no pop). Rules: pop starts only when visible (outside the 78-112 hero fade, so it is seen not wasted); flower pops rate-limited 6/s (burst 3); hysteresis open 0.5 / re-arm only below 0.3 AND faded out (decay never shrinks or re-pops; tested with 25 s fade and threshold jitter); reduced motion = instant, no sparkles; once open it joins the AL-1 sway unchanged. Caps reused: sway slots unchanged (64/36/0), sparkles use the existing CAPS ambient.bloom (High 32 / Med 16 / Low 0, phone x0.6), pooled additive 'glow' images at depth -349 (below every creature and the lightmap), cream only, alpha <= 0.55. Low: unchanged (no sway, no bloom, colour only). Visual only: own hashes, no sim RNG, sim untouched, sweeps wren+elias x24 seeds random+FOCUS byte-identical (cmp vs HEAD). tests/sway.test.mjs (+7), scripts/al2.mjs, docs/screenshots/al2/ (4-frame bud->pop strips + swarm frames). Perf 350 enemies: 60 fps all tiers (High work 8.9 vs 8.5 ms with sway off). Real-phone gate still outstanding. AL-3 not started.
- 2026-10-16 W2 GLOWING TRAILS BUILT on dev (visual only; awaiting Matt's look): src/gfx/ribbons.js (pure ring-buffer ribbons + RibbonSet segment emitter + pooled RibbonLayer of additive 'glow' sprites at depth 54000 = below every halo, creature, pickup and number). Hero lantern = warm cream-amber ribbon from the ember (High adds a thin cream core), scarf wisp (Wren pale scarf teal, Elias pale lavender trim), friendly spirits (wolves/ravens/orbit lights) keep their role colours; enemies and gold/evolution colours never used; they replace the old per-frame particle trails for those three (bolts keep theirs). Fade: ribbon life Low 1.0 s / Med 1.8 s / High 2.4 s for the lantern, scarf 0.7-0.9 s, spirits 0.7-0.8 s; the ~25 s mural is the existing life-map stamps (unchanged) so the ribbon is the bright head of it. Caps (CAPS.<tier>.ribbon): segments Low 12 / Med 96 / High 240, spirit ribbons Low 0 / Med 6 / High 12 (phone x0.6), Low = lantern only; reduced motion = lantern only, life x0.6, no core; ?trails=0 turns it off (dev). - 2026-10-16 W3 CORRUPTION BUILT on dev (Matt's go; visual only, awaiting his look): second float channel in the life map (src/world/lifemap.js `corr`, uploaded in the same 256x256 texture B channel at ~10 Hz, no extra texture/upload). Enemies stamp it at 10 Hz (r42, +0.011/tick; boss r130, +0.03), it fades on its own in ~60 s, and restored ground pushes it back (stamping is scaled down where life is high; decay +1.6/s x life), so lantern/trails/spirits heal it. Ground shader (Med/High): ink-dark ground (never red) with faint cold-violet cracks and a faint violet rim, noisy edge, static (no flashing); on-screen crack detail contrast p99 0.066 / p99.9 0.089 (limit 0.18, scripts/w3c.mjs contrast). Sway/bloom use `sampleClean` (life x (1 - corruption)) so they never appear on corrupted ground and return, blooming, when it heals. Cold enemy glow: faint steady additive blue-violet pool under enemies standing on ink (High 80 / Med 40 / none on Low, own pool under every creature). LOW fallback: up to 10 soft dark ink blobs under groups of 4+ enemies (no cracks, no glow), alpha follows the map. Fell Wolves/crawlers read well on the ink (red eyes + lighter bodies); numbers/pickups unchanged. Perf 350 enemies (1x): fps 59-60 all tiers, work Low/Med/High 6.4/8.3/9.4 ms (W2b 6.3/7.8/8.7); tickCorruption 0.25 ms per 10 Hz tick, life.update 0.9 -> 1.4 ms per 10 Hz tick. Sim untouched: sweeps wren+elias x24 seeds random+FOCUS byte-identical vs pristine HEAD. tests/lifemap.test.mjs (+4), tests/sway.test.mjs (+1), scripts/w3c.mjs, docs/screenshots/w3c/ (start / mid-swarm / restored-vs-corrupt boundary; desktop, phone landscape, phone portrait; High/Med/Low). ONE code-drawn atlas (src/gfx/propatlas.js, 2048 px, 3 texels/unit, Style v1 tinted outlines) with 11 prop kinds, each Hollow (grey, still) + restored (colour) sharing one silhouette box: Take A oak x2, snag x2 (blossom tips), bush x3, boulder x3, stump x2, log x2, grave x2, mushroom ring, bramble x2, fern x2; Take B bark pillar (750 design px tall, fades out at its top so it runs off any screen, + canopy ground-shadow decal, 1 variant + flip/size/tint jitter). - 2026-10-17 WL6c BUILT on dev (VIBRANCY of the restored world; visual only, sim untouched; spec visibility-arc-v1.md 2.2): vib = arc sat column (0.80 -> 1.30) x (0.85 + 0.15 x R), R = restored share of the view (mean sampleClean over a fixed 8 x 5 grid, sampled at 2 Hz, smoothed over 1.2 s; first tick snaps, no fade-in). Applied ONLY to restored-world layers: ground shader uniform `uSat` (saturation about luma of the RESTORED tile only, before the Hollow/restored mix, so Hollow and corruption are untouched; default 1.0 = authored look), sway tufts/flowers, restored prop images (`...r` layers only, never Hollow images or canopy shade), petals, critters, fireflies/motes (warm ones only: weighted by their restored tint index, cold Hollow ones unchanged). NEVER enemies, heroes, UI/HUD, pickups, numbers, telegraphs (tests pin the import list and that the enemy loop never mentions it). How sprites get it cheaply: multiply tints cannot saturate generally, so each atlas frame's mean colour is read once from its canvas (one readback per atlas, lazily, first use) and a per-frame tint c' = Y + s (c - Y) is computed on demand and cached per (frame, level) (17 levels 0.66..1.30, step 0.04); channels that would exceed white are normalised by the largest (luminance dip <= ~6% when boosting); desaturating sprites at night is compressed to 60% of the ground's (luminance dip <= ~15%, was 21%) to stay in the ground's luminance family (R1). Layer retints only when the quantised level changes (<= 2 Hz check), critters re-check per draw by (frame, level) key; no per-frame allocation (tests). Tiers: High and Med = ground uniform + sway + props + petals + critters + fireflies; LOW = TINT-ONLY on its <= 40 props (no ground shader, no sway/critters/fireflies on Low; the arc's Low tint already warms toward dawn); Brightness levels all respected (Normal/Brighter/Clear use the same vibrancy values, per the design sheet: Clear keeps the lifted-night LIGHTING, the vibrancy ramp still plays); Calm motion unaffected (colour, not motion). Dev param ?vib=0 = off (same-spot A/B). MEASURED (scripts/wl6-measure.mjs vib; real build, High, same spot with and without vibrancy, restored radius 330-620, mean chroma (max-min of RGB) of the central ground+props band): on/off ratio desk 4:00 0.88, 8:00 0.92, 11:30 0.95, 14:30 1.10; phone 4:00 0.87, 8:00 0.94, 11:30 0.99, 14:30 1.11 (absolute chroma off -> on at 14:30: 56.5 -> 62.2 desk, 58.0 -> 64.5 phone; measured below the theoretical 0.80/1.30 ratios because only the restored ground/props count, R < 1, and the dawn wash and clamp compress it). Enemy-interior chroma on vs off: within +-7% except one cell (14:30 phone, +15%, sample of 12 enemies with ground bleed at their edges; the code path never touches enemies, pinned by tests). Perf (pace.mjs, headless, 350 enemies, 15 s, same build ?vib=0 vs on, noisy machine): High work 12-14 vs 11-13 ms, Med 7-10 vs 8-10, Low 6-8 vs 5-10: no difference above the noise; alloc MB/s High 40.8 vs 37.9, Med 31.1 vs 30.8, Low 19.3 vs 19.0. Tests tests/vibrancy.test.mjs (12: maths, smoothness <= 1 level per second, satTint neutral/chroma/luma bounds, VibTints cache + heap, restoredShare, glow table, import-list wiring, restored-only props, shader order, no allocation, ?vib=0). Screenshots docs/screenshots/wl6c/ (meadow-<4m00|8m00|11m30|14m30>-<desk|port>-<vibon|viboff>.jpg, vibrancy.csv).

- 2026-10-17 W8b MARK STATUS + TOTAL DAMAGE-AMP CAP BUILT (v1.1 W8b; evolutions-second-review ruleset; Matt's go; awaiting his look): (1) MARK = +15% damage taken from ALL sources (every weapon, bleed/burn ticks, ability zones, bolts) for 3 s, REFRESH ONLY (re-marking resets the timer to 3 s, never stacks, never stronger), owner = the weapon acting when it is applied (counter markBy), bosses take Mark, a dead foe cannot be marked; sim-only state (never saved). Sim.mark(e) / e.markT / e.markSrc; constants MARK_AMP 0.15, MARK_TIME 3, AMP_CAP 1.75 exported from Sim.js. NO weapon applies it yet: base ravens do not mark; Talon Mark (W9) is the first applier. (2) TOTAL DAMAGE-AMP CAP: ONE clamp in the damage path (Sim.damageAmp, called by Sim.hurt): the PRODUCT of every temporary amp on the hit (Mark on the target x the source weapon's own amp in sim.srcAmps[weaponId], where the future Rally +50% will register as srcAmps.ghost_wolves = 1.5) is capped at x1.75; Rally 1.5 x Mark 1.15 = 1.725 passes unclamped, anything above 1.75 clamps. The amp is applied before the HP check and the counters. Counters: sim.ampBonus (extra damage dealt because of temporary amps), sim.markBy[weapon] (the Mark part). Rally is not built (W2 held on w2-rally) and the current Pack Howl x2 wolf bite is applied inside the wolf code, outside this clamp; Pack Howl is replaced by Rally in W2, which must register through srcAmps. (3) VISIBLE MARK: violet omen = a small violet chevron (dark tinted outline, ROLE.mark 0x9b7dff) bobbing over the head, blinking in the last 0.6 s as the expiry tell; shares the drip tier cap (High 40 / Med 24 / Low 0), not drawn under reduced motion; plus a static light violet body tint (ROLE.markTint 0xc9b8ff, all tiers incl. Low and reduced motion, never on bosses, which keep only the chevron at 2x size); distinct from Burn's rising lilac licks (shape) and Bleed's blood-red tint. Additive glow makes it read pale lilac on grass; real-build shots docs/screenshots/w8/ (*-mark.jpg zoom, *-mark-full.jpg at 1280x720 and 844x390). No damage-number tint (the amp is silent: the number simply reads 15% bigger). Tests tests/mark.test.mjs (11: constants, +15% from every source, DoT amped, no-stack/refresh, expiry at 3 s, boss ok, dead foe, cap at x1.75 incl. clamp and no leak to other weapons, counters, no-op without marks, kill after amp). (1) FIRST LIGHT 8:00-9:00 (envelope 7:45-9:45): fireflies drift up and fade, fog thins 40%, 1-3 sparrows glide in from the right, land (own world position), peck, and take off again (they leave early if the hero comes within 70); only plays if the run is inside 8:10-9:20. (2) THE HUSH ~10:00 (9:30 in, 10:25 out, 25 s hold): critters hush (CritterField.ext, they return after the normal 6 s calm), motes slow 60%, fireflies dim. (3) DAWN FLOOD 14:00-15:00 (burst fires once at 14:02): a staggered flock (cap = CAPS.ambient.birds 12/8/4, phone x0.6) takes flight from the lower left of the view up and to the right (view-anchored, sky layer, 5-9 s each, flapping the AL-5 sparrow frames), peach-cream petals drift (High 10 / Med 6 / Low 0), warm light shafts (High 3 / Med 2 / Low 0, additive 0.09 peak, quieter at Brighter x0.8 / Clear x0.65), critters come out eagerly (the calm timer is pre-satisfied), fireflies fade, fog thins 30%, and a soft COLOUR FLUSH (+0.05 saturation at full flood) is added to the restored-world vibrancy target (restored ground, tufts, props, critters, fireflies only; never enemies/pickups/UI). VICTORY: the final flush pulse (+0.12 saturation, ~4 s decay) plus a small flock (<= 6) if the sky is empty; results screen untouched. Calm motion = static: no birds, petals or shafts; only the colour flush, fade and thin remain; the arc dawn wash stays capped at 0.2. Low: 4 birds, no petals, no shafts. Everything is below every creature, pickup and number (birds/petals depth 4997-4998 under the lightmap, shafts 5001, creatures 55000+), own visual hash stream (seed ^ 0xda3c5, never the sim RNG), pooled images created on first use, typed arrays, zero per-frame allocation (tested). - 2026-10-17 W10b SPIRIT TEMPEST 'BURST AND SEEK' BUILT (spirit_tempest; id, recipe Bolt count + Swiftness and tree gate unchanged; awaiting Matt's look): every volley bursts a RING of 8 glyphs (45 degrees apart, flying straight for 0.22 s), then each is given its OWN prey: one claim set per volley, nearest unclaimed foe to that glyph among the 8 nearest within 240 px of you; the set is shared only when there are fewer foes than glyphs (evenly: at most one glyph difference per foe); a glyph whose prey dies takes the nearest foe no sister is chasing. NO blast in empty air (a glyph that fades pops nothing). Volley gap 0.85 s at Swiftness 5 (0.95 s at rank 0, -0.02 s per rank: the recipe maxes it, so 0.85 in play). Glyph speed x1.4, homing turn = Swiftness turn rate. Data keys in evolutions.json tune: dmg 1, cd 0.85, cdPer 0.02, speedK 1.4, burst 0.22, blast 0.12, blastR 22, each 1 (code fallbacks equal the data, B8 test). TWO DEVIATIONS FROM THE LOCKED NUMBERS, MATT CAN OVERRIDE: (1) the pop is 0.12x r22 on EVERY hit (a glyph with Pierce pops at each foe it passes), not 0.6x on the last hit. Why: with 0.6x on the last hit the glyph pops only when its pierce is spent, so a high-Pierce glyph never pops on a lone foe, and the pop scales with the crowd, not with Pierce (cap at pick 3.20x stream40 / 3.72x kite, OVER the 3x cap; blast 0.3x on the last hit still gives kite 3.02x). Per-hit 0.12x makes the evolution scale with Pierce like the base weapon does, so pick and max agree: cap at pick 2.49x kite / 2.21x stream40. ALTERNATIVES if Matt wants the locked verb back: (A) pop only on the last hit at 0.3x (kite max 1.83x, cap at pick kite 3.02x = at the cap, stream40 2.43x, single and boss unchanged, but a lone foe never gets a pop at max Pierce); (B) pop per hit at 0.2x (kite max 2.54x, over the 2.3x band). (2) boss 1.89x at max (locked estimate 2.0-2.3x; inside the general 1.8-2.3 band and never below base). MEASURED (scripts/evo-home.mjs 5 seeds, pick = pair 5 + others rank 1 | max): single 1.70x | 1.70x, swarm 4.94x | 2.00x (mortal swarm cap cell is above 3x at pick only; saturation rule: stream40 is 2.21x), KITE (home) 2.49x | 2.12x (band 1.8-2.3 ok), stream40 2.21x | 2.25x (<= 2.4 ok), boss 1.88x | 1.89x; pick rank 3: kite 2.16x, stream40 2.18x. Never below base >= 0.98x in single/stream10/stream40/swarm/kite/boss (test). Wasted glyphs on a lone foe 5% (<= 25%). No dead stat after evolving: Bolt power and Pierce each move crowd DPS > +10% (test); Pierce becomes a pop multiplier. VISUAL (render only, nothing in the sim): a cyan ring blooms from the hero (existing ring fx, capped at 48 live fx), a thin gold rim on the evolved glyph only (nearest caps.halos), a short thread (34 px, cap halos/5 = 8/18/32 by tier, none under reduced motion) from each seeking glyph toward its own prey; shots docs/screenshots/w10/tempest-* (true size 1280x720 + phone 844x390). CORRECTION to the W10a row: the boss figure there (64 -> 55) came from a probe that stalled at the first level-up; the harness-correct evo-home base boss DPS is 167 -> 164 (-2%) at max ranks, single 112 -> 165, swarm 695 -> 744, kite 638 -> 690, stream40 850 -> 870. Tests tests/bolts.test.mjs (+8: ring of 8, distinct claims, even sharing, no empty-air pop, gap 0.85/0.95, wasted <= 25%, no dead stat, determinism, band + cap@pick gates) and evo-promises cap@pick now covers Spirit Tempest. | 2026-10-17 | docs/design/evolutions-second-review.md 4.3, scripts/evo-home.mjs, tests/bolts.test.mjs |

- 2026-10-17 W12 VESPER TIDE BUILT (id vesper_tide, Holy Orbit third evolution, recipe Orbit size + Orbit speed, late tree gate 16 = evolve Holy Orbit 3 times + play 8 runs as Elias, 600 gold, ring 3 / ang 21; counts estimated). Ring radius R(t) = 14 + (1.7 x Orbit size - 14) x (0.5 - 0.5 cos phi), phi rate = PHASE x Orbit speed; hits made while the ring pushes OUT (sin phi > 0) deal x2.0 (crest), drawing in x1.0; Orbit count = lights, power = damage, size = swell reach, speed = breathing rate and hit rate (D1 holds). GUARD PRIORITY: the W4 guard takes the breathing radius as its input, so a foe touching Elias closes the ring to 14 px (0.25 s) whatever the phase, the closing/closed ring never gets the crest, and the tide resumes when the guard opens. Burn as locked (every Orbit hit, crest hits carry x2 potency, share 19-24% in the hug/boss probe, <= 35%); no breathing, no crest and no Burn while the Candle Wheel stands. ONE NUMBER DIFFERS FROM THE ESTIMATE: PHASE = 1.5 (locked row said 1.0 x Orbit speed). Measured on the BUILT W4+Burn base (evo-home, 5 seeds, pick = pair 5 + others 1, mortal): at 1.0 the evolution misses (kite 1.44x, swarm 0.96x BELOW base, stream40 1.18x, boss 1.05x); crest alone saturates (crest 3-4 kite 1.64x at 16 seeds, swarm 0.99x: kills cap the gain, coverage is the limit); PHASE 1.5 with crest 2.0 gives kite 1.87x (16 seeds 1.94x), swarm 1.04x (1.06x), stream40 1.24x (1.26x), boss 1.04x, single INF (base ring never reaches the dummy; evo 106 DPS), cap@pick kite 2.21x / stream40 1.29x / swarm 1.33x / boss 1.06x (all <= 3x), never below base >= 0.98x in every scenario. ALTERNATIVE if Matt wants the locked 1.0x breathing back: set tune.phase = 1 (data only) and accept kite ~1.4x and swarm ~0.96x, or add crest 2.5 + phase 1.3 (kite ~2.0x). Card text 'The ring swells out and draws back, striking hard on every swell.' (66 chars). Visuals (render hooks only): thin gold rim on the evolved ring only, soft lilac halo that swells with the ring, short bright streak on each light while it pushes out, fading ripple left at the crest as it draws back; Calm = rim only (no halo growth, no streaks, no ripple, damage unchanged); Low = no streaks/ripple. Tests tests/vesper-tide.test.mjs (9), evo-promises cap@pick (single has base 0: absolute cap), gate/evolution count tests 16 / 15 / 13 gated. Shots docs/screenshots/w12/, probe scripts/w4-hug.mjs + scripts/w12-shots.mjs.

- 2026-10-17 RELEASE GATE (Matt): NOTHING merges to main / goes live until the whole current game is 'done and dusted' and as good as it can be. REQUIRED before first public launch (all chosen by Matt): (1) R1 retune (both heroes in the 15-30% no-tree band, one pass over everything, Rally merged); (2) ENEMIES: design sheet #4 -> lock -> build (roster, minibosses/chests, corruption bloom, eyes-only night look); (3) SKILL TREE: structure v2 (per-weapon emblem constellations, three evolution sockets per weapon, gates for all 15 evolutions, status power nodes), look, desktop + phone: design -> lock -> build; (4) BOSS: Elder Treant 3-phase redesign sheet -> lock -> build; (5) AUDIO: sound palette + music direction sheet -> lock -> build (free/code-generated sources only, licences recorded); (6) FRONT DOOR: device profiles (max 3) + Continue/New + map select; (7) FIRST-RUN TUTORIAL/onboarding hints (short, skippable); (8) final verification: full security review + npm audit, real-phone test (shader, frame rate, controls), name check (itch.io/Steam/UK IPO), CREDITS + AI disclosure, full balance sweep, visual QA at all sizes, friend playtest via a private link, Matt's playtest checklist. Merge to main only with Matt's explicit yes after all are done. Order: R1 -> enemies (design in parallel with R1) -> skill tree -> boss -> front door + tutorial -> audio -> final verification -> private test -> merge.

- 2026-10-17 RELEASE GATE ADDITIONS (Matt: 'a first-time player should think wow, a lot of thought went into this'): added to required-before-launch: (9) FIRST-60-SECONDS + GAME-FEEL pass (opening minute design + hit/kill/gem/level-up/phone-vibration feel); (10) SUMMONS ART sheet #3 (wolves, ravens, glyphs, lights, lightning in Style v1, three options each) -> lock -> build; (11) CODEX + UNLOCK CELEBRATIONS (bestiary/evolution codex screen, first-unlock celebrations for tree nodes/weapons/evolutions); (12) POST-VICTORY REPLAY LOOP (harder tiers or endless dusk after clearing Chapter 1). Also folded into final polish by default (Matt may veto): logo + app/favicon/install icons + share-preview image; About page (credits, licences, AI disclosure, version, feedback link); accessibility basics (text size, remappable keys, gamepad on PC, reduced motion exists, colour-blind checks exist); save safety (backup reminder, corruption recovery); low-end phone pass (battery/heat/offline/update).

- 2026-10-17 EXTENSIBILITY PRINCIPLE (Matt): get the launch foundation right so later content (maps, chapters, weapons, heroes, enemies) is cheap to add. Therefore, as part of the release gate, build a CONTENT PIPELINE: (a) everything content stays data-first (JSON + validators + tests), chapters/maps/zones/enemy rosters/waves/bosses defined as data with a chapter template; (b) templates + validators for weapon (4 stats x 5 ranks, hint templates), evolution (recipe pair, three per weapon, codex line, cue, gate), hero (kit, ability, rig params), enemy family (rig, eyes, tier caps) and chapter/map (zones, props sets, ARC table, ground tiles, POIs); (c) a docs/CONTENT-GUIDE.md ('how to add X' checklists with the measurement standard, gates, screenshots, security) and scripts that scaffold + validate a new piece; (d) per-chapter art/palette/ARC parameters, not hard-coded to Chapter 1; (e) the three-options-Matt-picks process for each new piece. Scheduled after the skill tree/boss builds and before final verification.

- 2026-10-18 SKILL TREE EXTENSIBILITY (Matt): the tree must be EASY TO ADD TO later (new weapons, heroes, chapters, status nodes, evolutions). Requirements for T1-T7: (a) fully data-driven nodes/clusters/rings (JSON) with a documented schema + validator; (b) a CLUSTER TEMPLATE for a new weapon (emblem path, node slots, three evolution sockets, status nodes) so adding a weapon = data + one emblem drawing, no screen code changes; (c) layout is data-driven (positions per cluster with an auto-layout helper/check for overlaps, label collisions, reachability) and the fogged future-chapter band + reserved ring slots exist from day one; (d) ids permanent and namespaced per weapon/hero/chapter, versioned save migrations tested with frozen price tables, hostile-save tests; (e) the soft-cap/PE budget is computed from data with a CI-style test that FAILS when a new node pushes the sum over the ceiling (and a documented process to raise the ceiling deliberately); (f) docs/CONTENT-GUIDE.md section 'How to add to the skill tree' + a scaffold/validate script; (g) performance plan holds at 500+ nodes (culling/pooling).
- 2026-10-18 SKILL TREE GROWTH (Matt): after v1 the tree will expand A LOT (many more weapons, heroes, chapters, statuses). It is built with that in mind from T1: design targets 1000+ nodes (not just 500): virtualised/culled rendering with a spatial index, semantic zoom levels (overview shows clusters/emblems only; nodes/labels appear as you zoom), search/filter + minimap + bookmarks/fast-travel to a cluster, region/chapter-scoped lazy loading of node data, compact save format (ownership stored compactly, e.g. per-cluster arrays/bitsets with id maps) and fast migrations, per-chapter/per-cluster soft-cap budgets and category caps that scale (so the global ceiling is recalculated by rule, not hand-edited), stable namespaced ids, and performance budgets (frame time, memory, save size, load time) with tests at 1000 and 2000 synthetic nodes. No hard-coded counts, positions or cluster lists in screen code.

- 2026-10-18 EN0 BUILT on dev (enemy art pipeline; render only, sim untouched): src/gfx/husk/paint.js + kit.js (the locked mock-up painter ported 1:1; grain filter region bounded to the frame box, eye() records socket anchors) -> husks.js (rigs) -> frames.js (data: frames walk x4 / tell / hurt / freed body / mask per type, frame box, world scale g/4, foot offset, per-frame eye-slot anchors, per-id jitter) -> src/gfx/enemytex.js bakes ONE chapter atlas 'en_atlas' at boot from our own SVG sheets (Blob URL -> img.decode -> canvas; one type per slice; no external refs, no untrusted input), 1024 x 394 (frames 1.10 MB incl. half-density night-rim frames; 1.6 MB GPU), bake 0.8-1.3 s async on the headless harness: the title is NOT blocked (only ?auto runs wait, max 4 s; flat sprites until the atlas lands; ?husks=0 dev switch). G3 blue floor: opaque pixels get B >= 48 (cold indigo blacks) so no enemy pixel falls under RGB 12. src/gfx/enemyrig.js: one pooled image per enemy, frame swap only, animation state on the pooled image (no per-frame allocation), Low = 2-frame walk. Night layer: eyes read per-frame socket anchors (x jitter scale), rims are atlas outline frames per walk/tell frame. Husk eye image = WL6b size x HUSK_EYE_K (wolf 0.6, crow 0.9, crawler 0.85) so the cream core sits in the socket (at full size the ember halo hid the whole mask): A WL6b SIZE DEVIATION, NEEDS MATT'S OK. Husk types skip the old dark-type tint floor (it was for the near-black crawler sprite and made the pale-masked Rootback match bright ground); crow body x0.85 once bodyK < 1. Death beat: freed body (mask off, pale muzzle, closed eye) sinks 0.5 s, mask pops forward, tumbles, lands and fades (Med/High), a warm gold mote rises (every kill, all tiers); caps 40/20/8, phone x0.6, Calm = no tumble spin. - 2026-10-18 LAG + WALK FIX (bugfix, render/frame-timing only; Matt: PC 'laggy, struggling to load things in', walk 'not smooth, head bouncing'): CAUSES FOUND: (1) camera startFollow(roundPixels=true) floored the camera scroll AND every sprite to whole WORLD units (= 2-4 device px at our zoom): the world juddered in uneven 1/2-unit steps and each hero rig part snapped on its own; (2) Phaser's 10-frame delta smoothing turned each dropped frame into slow-motion + catch-up; (3) walk cycle: rectified-sine bob (corner at every contact) + 9% stretch = ~9.5 du head travel, head squashed with the body, feet lifted while sliding BACK, breathing/tilt snapped on at the 'moving' threshold, lean flipped instantly, ember spring (k900 d0.5) resonated at the step rate; (4) Deep Forest tile bake read pixels back from GPU canvases (~2.5 s of dropped frames at run start) and uploaded 4 MB in a slice frame; (5) the weapon strip's first fade rasterized the strip mid-play (~100 ms GPU hitch at ~8 s); (6) DEV ONLY: Vite HMR full-reloaded Matt's running game whenever an agent saved a src file. FIXES: src/gfx/camfollow.js (float, frame-rate independent 0.18/60 Hz, device-pixel snapped scroll); fps.smoothStep off; WALK v2 in rigmath (walkPose/walkDrive/turnSquash/easeTo: one smooth bump per step, body lowest just after contact, head lags a beat and never squashes, feet lift on the forward swing with sin^2, phase from smoothed speed, stride shortens when slow, amount eases in/out, lean eases through turns + small accel lean, 0.14 s turn squeeze; ember k220 d0.72 with a velocity lead; Wren + Elias; Calm/reduced = static as before; Low flat sprite unchanged); B tiles on a CPU canvas, upload in its own frame, painted on the title screen; .h-strip will-change: opacity; vite dev HMR off unless HMR=1. - 2026-10-18 SKILL TREE T2 BUILT (save version 3 + Regrowth; tests/savev3.test.mjs; docs/design/skill-tree-v2.md 7b 'How saves migrate'): SAVE_VERSION 3. On disk the tree is COMPACT (src/meta/treecodec.js): tree = {v: treeVersion, o: {fragment key: base64url bitset by idx}, r: {id: rank>1}}, seen = {o: {...}}, fragment key = the fragment's prefix (core, wf, rv, bo, cl, or, wr, el); in memory the game still holds {id: rank} / {id: true} (UI and sim unchanged); serializeSave writes it for autosave, backup and Export; sanitize() accepts either form. MIGRATION REGISTRY (src/meta/migrations/): envelope 2 -> 3 runs the tree step 'Regrowth' (tree-v3.js) once, before sanitising: every non-permanent owned node is cleared and refunded at its OLD price from a deep-frozen table (tree-v3.prices.js, 82 ids with maxRank, rankCost, shards, permanent flag p x16, teaser flag t x3; asserted equal to the committed v1 tree), ranks clamped to the old maxRank, gold/shards clamped 1e12; the 16 permanent gate nodes stay owned and refund nothing (a lone Talon Mark without the Raven heart stays owned); unknown ids and teasers vanish; idempotent (second pass refunds 0; a v3 envelope never re-enters; a forged low tree.v is clamped up to 3 so it cannot replay the refund; tree.v above the data = refused as 'future'); newer tree versions run TREE_STEPS above the stored version (tested with a fake step 4). Additive content needs NO step (tested: a whole extra cluster added to the data loses nothing from a v3 save and the bytes on disk do not change). New bounded fields: notes.regrowth {gold, shards, nodes} (one-time notice for the T4 tree screen) and stats.recentRuns (last 10 runs {t, k kills by enemy <= 12 keys, e <= 8 evolution ids, b boss, c clear}; bankRun appends it). HOSTILE-INPUT TESTS: prototype keys (__proto__/constructor/prototype) in tree, o, r, notes, recentRuns; non-string / non-base64url / padded / 6000-char bitsets; unknown fragment keys; stray high bits; ranks without bits, above max, NaN, strings; 20000-key objects (capped at 4096 keys, < 200 ms); junk shapes for tree/seen/stats; 200 KB size cap; 60000-deep nesting; gold at 1e12 ceiling. SIZE/SPEED at 2000 owned nodes (synthetic): tree part 1613 bytes, seen 1600 bytes (budget 2 KB each), parseSave ~15 ms idle (target 20 ms; test allows 200). Existing tests deliberately updated: T16 (in-memory save version 3) and the v2-save migration test (now a Regrowth: 300 gold back for two Hearth lanterns). Gap noted (not changed): stats.evolved is capped at 64 keys by the old save rule, enough for the planned ~30 evolutions. No balance change: sweeps byte-identical (see T1 row). | 2026-10-18 | src/save/schema.js, src/meta/migrations/, src/meta/treecodec.js |
- 2026-10-18 DIFFICULTY TIERS IDEA (Matt; this IS the 'post-victory replay loop' release-gate item 12, expanded): each map/chapter has NORMAL / HARD / EXPERT (names provisional) difficulty levels; higher tiers are UNLOCKED through the skill tree; harder tiers pay MORE resources, including possibly NEW resources used to unlock further tree bits; some tree upgrades are LOCKED behind having completed certain tier activities (e.g. clear a map on Hard); must be carefully balanced with the skill tree economy (gold/soul shards/new resource) and the soft-cap power budget (higher tiers must not become the only way to reach power: the budget ceiling still holds). NEEDS: research (how Vampire Survivors, Spirit Hunters, Brotato (danger levels), Halls of Torment, Hades (Heat), Dead Cells (Boss Cells), Rogue Legacy/Vampire Survivors stage unlocks handle tiers/modifiers/currency), then a design sheet with THREE options per piece (tier structure and modifiers (enemy hp/speed/density, new enemy modifiers, fewer Blessings, boss changes), the new resource(s) and sinks, unlock path in the tree, reward scaling, replay hooks (first-clear bonuses, per-tier records), UI (map select shows tiers, locked tiers teaser)), a balance plan (bot per tier targets: e.g. Normal 15-30% starter, Hard / Expert win bands for a mature toolkit), save implications (per-map-per-tier records, versioned), extensibility (tiers as data per chapter/map). SEQUENCING: design BEFORE tree T3 (rescale) and T4/T5 (screen) because it changes the economy and tree layout (a 'Difficulty' ring/branch in the Lantern Web). Not built before the design is locked.
- 2026-10-18 TREE UPGRADE GROUPS (Matt: 'I want the skill tree to feel like it actually gives useful upgrades to progression as well as useful quality-of-life upgrades, e.g. XP collection distance'): ALL FOUR groups are included in the skill tree redesign (each with small capped effects inside the soft-cap budget; QoL first/cheap, power-adding later/expensive): (1) COLLECTION & PICKUP: XP gem pickup range (several small steps), gem pull speed, gems fly to you on level-up, better heart/magnet drops or shorter pity timer; (2) CARDS & INFORMATION: 4th card on level-up, peek at the next offer, lock a favourite weapon (biases stat cards), stronger 'something stirs' evolution hint, enemy health bars, chest/miniboss radar; (3) MASTERY & BESTIARY: slay-count bonuses vs specific enemy types (e.g. 1000 crows: +2% vs crows), per-weapon and per-hero mastery, bestiary completion rewards; (4) RUN START & SAFETY: choose starting weapon, 5th weapon slot, starting Blessing, one revive per run, ability auto-use thresholds and cooldown reduction (floor 30 s: already planned). These go into the difficulty-tier + economy design pass (three options per group, node inventory, budget table) before tree T3. Existing Blessings (pickup radius etc.) must not double-count with tree nodes (global caps in blessings.json caps and the tree soft cap).
- 2026-10-18 TREE SIZE AND RESOURCES (Matt): the skill tree may be AS BIG as it needs to be and use AS MANY RESOURCES as needed (no artificial node or currency cap), ALWAYS with balance kept in mind (soft-cap/PE budget rules, economy sizing, per-tier reward balance) and ALWAYS with the right design (each resource has a clear identity, source and sinks; the screen stays readable and fun at any size via the Lantern Web semantic zoom/search/minimap; no padding nodes). Each new resource needs: a thematic fit, where it drops, what it buys, an economy line in the balance table, and a save field with validation.

- 2026-10-18 EYES IN SOCKETS BUILT on dev (Matt: 'the eyes look like a random red glowing ball in the face'; render only, sim untouched). SUPERSEDES the EN0 default 'Husk night eyes at 0.6-0.9x the WL6b size' (HUSK_EYE_K removed). DIAGNOSIS: the atlas frames carried EMPTY sockets and the night layer drew the WL6b one-image eye (ember disc + cream core) at a fixed anchor: 5.4 wu on the wolf and 9.6 wu on the crow vs sockets of 1.3-2.2 wu, i.e. a disc 2.7-5x the socket floating over the mask, flat red, no depth, no lid, same circle for every species. BUILD: (1) eyes BAKED INTO every walk/tell/hurt frame of each species' socket (paint.js eye(): knotSocket() records each socket outline, the eye is clipped to it): ember iris (cream -> red-orange -> deep red edge), species core (wolf narrow slit slanted -14 deg, crow one big round core with a white highlight, crawler a wide low oval on two wide-set sockets), dark brow lid over the top 24% (hurt: both lids, squint), inner shadow ring (depth), warm catch-light on the lower bark lip; the freed body and the fallen mask have none (death = the eyes go out with the mask). (2) NIGHT glow = socket-shaped additive atlas frames per socket, attached to the same per-frame anchors and the instance scale: 'g' ember fill clipped to the socket + faint lip spill, 'c' core, 'G' glow+core in one image, 'h'/'H' hot versions used only while the High camera bloom is on (the bloom mixes 55% of a blurred frame and halved a 3-5 px eye). The nearest CORE_CAPS enemies (High 40 / Med 20 / Low 0) get a separate core that drifts toward the hero inside the socket's free room (per-socket clamp, tested on every frame); all others and all of Low draw ONE glow+core image per eye (so eye image count = the old one-image eyes). Blink = the lid closes (sprites squash to 15%, never leave the socket), hurt = squint (core 55%, glow 85%, hotter), tell = flare (+30% alpha, core +25%), closing in = core grows up to +25%, glow <= +9%; streak thinner (socket height). Calm = no drift/pulse/blink (unchanged). Eye caps 200/120/40 unchanged. (3) Daylight dark outline (bodyK < 1, Med/High, rim caps): crow 0.6, crawler 0.5, wolf 0.35 from the atlas outline frames, tint 0x1c2034 (fixes the 3 EN2 dawn misses); plus a night pool outline on the crow (0.5, lit >= 12/16, only while the High bloom is on). (4) Harness fix (scripts/wl6-measure.mjs): POS also ends the Husk hurt frame (the harness holds the sim clock, so one hit while staging kept the 0.14 s squint on forever). RESULTS: contrast full matrix (4860 cells, docs/screenshots/eyes/contrast/) G1|G2 4857/4860 (EN2 4846; normal vision 1215/1215; the 3 misses are the known WL6d root crawler 220 portrait protan/tritan daylight cells, eye 2.87-2.99:1), the previous build of this chunk measured 4860/4860 (run noise about +-3 cells); G3 0 px; all dawn cells pass. PERF pace.mjs headless High 350 enemies, 4 alternating pairs vs the pre-chunk build: work 10.98 -> 11.15 ms avg (+0.17, noise +-0.3), draw section 4.09 -> 4.14 ms. (A first version with a separate core on every eye cost +1.7 ms and was replaced by the CORE_CAPS split.) Atlas 1024 x 394 -> 1024 x 438 (frames 1.22 MB). Sweeps wren + elias x 24 random + FOCUS byte-identical vs a pristine worktree of 7704529 (4/4); git diff src/sim empty; HUD computed-style hash identical before/after at 3 sizes; npm test 765 green; audit 0. - 2026-10-18 DIFFICULTY TIERS + TREE UPGRADES + ECONOMY (docs/design/difficulty-tiers-economy-v1.md, docs/research/39-difficulty-tiers-economy.md, docs/mockups/difficulty-tiers/) decisions by Matt, all option A: (1) THREE FIXED TIERS per map: Twilight / Deep Dusk / Long Night (names provisional; Deep Dusk enemy HP x1.45, dmg x1.15, boss HP x1.5; Long Night HP x2.0, dmg x1.3, boss x2.0, hearts drop more slowly; each harder tier adds one readable rule: 'Hollow marks' affixes with one verb each (Ironbark, Swiftshade, Emberwake, Hollowheart), read by shape first; Blessings, tree and the 15-minute run are the same on every tier); (2) ONE NEW RESOURCE DAWNGLASS (peach-to-cyan, not gold): drops only on the two harder tiers (boss, victory, first-clear bounty per hero per tier, capped marked-foe drops, per-run cap); buys Fourth Card, Fifth Hook (5th weapon slot), the Long Night gate, a keystone pair, cosmetics; no conversion between currencies; (3) TIER POWER: Fourth Card and Fifth Hook (tier-bought power) apply ONLY on the tiers that pay it (Deep Dusk / Long Night), so Twilight keeps the 15-30% starter band and the ceiling. UNLOCK PATH: new Night Gate cluster in the tree: Deep Dusk after one Twilight clear (1,200 gold + 2 shards), Long Night after one Deep Dusk clear (2,500 gold + 4 shards + 10 Dawnglass); 3 stars per map-tier. TOOL CLUSTERS (four upgrade groups + Night Gate = five clusters, 54 lanterns: 43 new + 11 existing ids moved, ids unchanged): Satchel (collection), Card Fan (cards and info), Almanac (bestiary slay-count lanterns +1% then +2% vs enemy type), Hearth-key (run start and safety), placed on the five far slots between weapon constellations (reserved future-weapon slots drop 10 -> 5 plus 2 per chapter band). BLESSING NO-DOUBLE-COUNT: one combined cap per shared effect (pickup 1.45, gold 1.40, speed 1.08, max HP 1.18, damage 1.09); a Blessing leaves the pool once its effect is at cap. BUDGET: Twilight ceiling holds: combat PE at cap 15.32 -> 15.18 (limit 15.5; new lore +1.2, choice +0.8 paid by trimming six category caps by 2.14), sustain stays exactly 5.0; Deep Dusk +2.5 PE only on that tier, Long Night +0.6 more. ECONOMY (model, bot-measure in DT8): whole tree ~110,850 gold + 43 shards + 46 Dawnglass; first Twilight clear ~3 runs, whole tree ~48 bot / 64 human runs. Open weak spots to measure: first Deep Dusk clear ~27 bot/35 human runs (target 15-25), Fifth Hook may not add power for random pickers, tier settings depend on the miniboss/chest/boss redesign not built yet. BUILD: DT0 inside tree T3, DT1..DT9 per the sheet. Matt gave the open ideas S1-S8 (daily seed, optional risk toggles, endless mode, hero trials, Twilight assist, cosmetics, nudges, tier-tinted world colour) as suggestions only: NOT included unless asked.
- 2026-10-18 EXTRA IDEAS INCLUDED IN V1 (Matt: 'I like your ideas too'; chosen from the suggestions S1-S8 in docs/design/difficulty-tiers-economy-v1.md): (1) COSMETICS: lantern colours and trail styles (gold/Dawnglass/achievement unlocks; non-power; solves 'gold has nothing to buy after the tree'); (2) TWILIGHT ASSIST options (optional comfort settings on the easiest tier, e.g. slower enemies / more health; disables record stars; accessibility); (3) DAILY SEED + ENDLESS DUSK MODE (fixed seed per day with a local best, endless mode after a win; all local, no server); (4) OPTIONAL RISK TOGGLES / curses (extra hardships for bonus Dawnglass). NOT chosen (stay parked): hero trials, tier-tinted world colour, nudges beyond the map-select next-goal line (S5/S7/S8 unless asked). BALANCE RULES: none of these may add power outside the budget: cosmetics = zero power; assist = difficulty-lowering only with stars disabled; daily/endless = no extra permanent power or capped records; curses add Dawnglass income so the economy table MUST be re-modelled (cap curse income per run, anti-farm) and measured by the bot (DT8). Each goes through a short design pass with THREE options per piece (three-options rule) BEFORE build; the existing tree needs no extra POWER nodes (budget full: combat 15.18/15.5, sustain 5.0/5.0): extra nodes are cosmetic/stars/QoL only; if harder tiers measure too hard, tune tier rules/tier-scoped power (+2.5 PE Deep Dusk, +0.6 Long Night) first.
- 2026-10-18 TREE PULL PRINCIPLE (Matt: 'I want the skill tree to feel like you actually want to fill out everything: players want to play hard levels, other characters or weapons, AND want to keep picking up power nodes; eventually HUGE so there is plenty of replayability'): the tree is designed as a multi-axis pull. ALL FOUR mechanisms are in: (1) GOAL TRACKER: pin up to 3 goals with progress on the tree and the results screen + ECONOMY PACING RULE: after any run something is affordable or nearly affordable (a node within ~2 runs at every stage of the economy; measured by the bot in DT8; guard against dead zones); (2) MULTI-AXIS UNLOCKS: many nodes require different activities (a Deep Dusk/Long Night clear, a win with a given hero, an evolution with each weapon, enemy slay-counts) so chasing nodes sends players to other tiers, heroes and weapons; (3) FOG TEASERS: silhouettes of future weapon constellations and the future chapter band so the tree always shows more to discover; (4) COMPLETION REWARDS + 'THE HOLLOW REMEMBERS': finishing a cluster lights its whole emblem and awards a title + cosmetic; the hub lantern brightens with total progress; the title/menu backdrop regains colour as the tree fills (meta restoration; visual only). Design implications: add to tree T4/T5 (screen: goal tracker, completion lighting, fog teasers) and to the economy model (pacing rule, no dead zones, no grind walls), with a hidden/unfair-lock check (every locked node shows its condition and progress); also extend the Lantern Web content over time (future chapters/weapons/heroes slot into reserved far slots and fog bands).
- 2026-10-18 TREE NODE STATE VISIBILITY (Matt): nodes that can be unlocked RIGHT NOW (prerequisites met AND all costs affordable in every required resource: gold, shards, Dawnglass etc.) are shown in a distinct, instantly recognisable colour/glow so the next unlock is visible at a glance without searching; as resources are spent the highlight disappears from nodes that are no longer affordable (live update, also after a run banks resources). States (T4/T5 design): OWNED (lit), AFFORDABLE NOW (the new highlight: bright lantern glow ring + subtle pulse, reduced-motion = static), AVAILABLE BUT NOT YET AFFORDABLE (prerequisites met, shows what is missing e.g. '120 gold short', calmer tint), LOCKED (condition + progress shown, dim), and hidden/fogged teasers. Colour from the Lantern Glass kit tokens, distinct from teal NEW and gold evolution seals, colour-blind safe (also a shape/ring cue, not colour alone). Extras: a 'show only affordable' filter/toggle, a counter badge ('4 unlockable'), jump-to-next-affordable button, and the pinned goals from the goal tracker. Multi-resource costs: affordable only when ALL costs are met.
- 2026-10-18 SKILL TREE = TOP PRIORITY AFTER CORE GAMEPLAY (Matt: 'other than the actual design and gameplay the skill tree is one of the most important things to get right as it gives players something to chase: it needs to be right and impactful; sometimes more is better'). Therefore extra quality gates before the tree is called done: (1) PROGRESSION TIMELINE SIMULATION: simulate a player's first ~100 runs (bot-based + human-pace model) and chart what is affordable, what they are chasing and what they unlock at each point; fix dead zones/grind walls/too-fast completion; (2) INDEPENDENT 'CHASE' REVIEW (fresh strongest-model agent) judging desirability: at each stage what does the player WANT next, are rewards impactful and felt (each purchase changes how a run plays or feels, not just +1%), is there enough content (more is better: err on the side of MORE meaningful nodes, each with a clear effect, never padding); (3) INTERACTIVE PROTOTYPE of the new tree screen with real data (pan/zoom/tap/affordable highlights/goal tracker/fog teasers) for Matt to try on PC and phone BEFORE the full T4/T5 build is locked; (4) Matt's tree playtest checklist (does it make you want to fill it out, is the next unlock obvious, does buying feel good) at the end. Impact rule: every purchasable node must have a player-visible effect or unlock (clear text, number, preview); big milestones (keystones, tier gates, weapon awakenings, cluster completions) get a celebration moment (hook for the codex/unlock celebration chunk).

DECISION: raise the combat ceiling about 2x and re-balance around it ('it should feel impactful; sometimes more is better'); the no-tree Twilight starter band 15-30% is UNAFFECTED (tree power only applies when nodes are owned); sustain ceiling to be raised only as far as measurements justify (propose, keep revives/heal bounded); re-balance enemy scaling / tier rules for stronger mature toolkits in the FINAL retune; per-weapon pools and category caps scale by rule (budget test snapshot updated deliberately; documented rule for future growth: per-weapon pool, per-chapter allowance); drop/merge dead padding nodes (54 flagged); node impact rule: every purchasable node must change something visible. Targets after the change (bot, 96 seeds, Blessings on): full v2 tree both heroes 45-55% random / 65-75% FOCUS, Wren-Elias gap <= 5 (any residual hero-baseline gap handled by hero-side tuning in the final retune, not by tree asymmetry). - 2026-10-20 WOLF SPREAD + LEASH FIX (bug vs the locked "wolves hunt DIFFERENT targets"; Matt's playtest: "7 wolves go for the same enemy"). CAUSE (probe scripts/wolf-leash-probe.mjs, per-frame distinct targets / min(wolves, foes within 150 of the hero), no Rally): the old pick only searched foes within 170 of the WOLF and 150 of the hero, then fell back to ANY claimed foe, so a pack of 6-7 shared: 70-77% distinct with enough foes (57-61% with few), 2-3 wolves 93-99%. RULE (src/sim/weapons.js wolfPick): candidates = foes within 150 of the hero (leash), each wolf takes the unclaimed foe with the best score (distance from the wolf + distance from the hero, so near-hero foes win and a far shooter or tank does not drag the pack off); widen to 185 only when the whole leash is claimed; only when nothing is free does a wolf share, joining the LEAST-claimed foe (even spread over few foes; a lone boss still takes the whole pack); a target past 200 of the hero is dropped at once; Rally unchanged (converges). RESULT: distinct ratio with enough foes 88/90/86% -> 98.8% (all packs, 72 seeds); wolf >150 px from the hero 9% -> 11% of wolf time (mean distance 92 -> 96 px; the first, looser version without the 185/200 limits hit 24%). The Husk-chase theory was wrong: wolves spend only ~5% of their time on the three new types; the Wren loss is the new enemies' pressure itself (control with the three waves removed = 24% at 96 seeds). BALANCE (96 seeds, random/FOCUS): WREN 11/8% -> 6/5%, ELIAS 17/19% -> 17/23%. Spreading the pack costs Wren about 5 points (focus fire killed faster); Wren starter is now BELOW the 15% band; levers for the FINAL retune (not applied): Wren base bite/Pack numbers, enemy pressure (piper/hart caps), hpScale. EVO-HOME (3 seeds, pick 2): Light Trails kite cap@pick 2.73x -> 3.39x (breached the hard 3x) because the base got weaker while trails ride wolf paths: trailHit 0.24 -> 0.16 (kite cap 3.01x, home swarm 1.96x, kite 2.54x; was 2.02/2.38); Echo Pack home swarm 1.91->1.96x, kite 1.97->2.18x; The Alpha swarm 2.37->2.52x, kite 1.66->1.83x (all in the 1.8-2.3x band or just over; cap <= 3x). Lone-boss wolf DPS 274 -> 231 (wolves now also bite adds); ravens-vs-wolves test bound 1.15 -> 1.35. Tests: tests/wolf-spread.test.mjs (5), lightfoot test constant. | 2026-10-20 | src/sim/weapons.js, src/data/evolutions.json, tests/wolf-spread.test.mjs, scripts/wolf-leash-probe.mjs |

(4) DEATH RECAP (default taken by Claude, Matt may override): option A 'Struck down by a Fell Wolf's lunge' + 'What hurt you' bars (uses the existing damage-by-source counters; results screen one-screen rule holds). - 2026-10-20 BOSS DIRECTION (Matt's playtest; REOPENS the locked 'boss Elder Treant at 10:00' line; feeds design sheet #7): (1) TIMING: the Elder Treant comes at the VERY END of the 15-minute run: you either beat it or die when time runs out (no 'survive to dawn' without killing it); the run structure becomes: build up for ~12+ min, then the boss fight at the end; the visibility arc/dawn flood must align (the boss fight happens at dawn light; revisit WL6 'boss at 10:00 in first grey light 1.32x' and AL-6 dawn chorus timing: first light ~8:00-9:00, hush ~10:00, flood from 14:00 are re-timed in the boss design); minibosses at 3:20/7:00/12:30 (Branch-Mane) must be re-timed so they do not collide with the boss; (2) OWN ARENA: when the boss appears it gets its own arena that appears on screen so the player CANNOT run away: forced to fight; the arena must be big enough that the player can still run/kite around inside it (not a tiny box); designed in the boss sheet (arena shape/size, boundary look (roots/thorn wall/colour-returns ring), what happens to regular enemies (cleared/stopped/continue), camera, mobile view, pickups/XP); (3) MOVES: the boss needs its own varied moves, not just 'dodge touching it': three-options design per move set, 3 phases, telegraphs per the new readability rules (shared telegraph style, bright only for real threats), eye/visibility rules, First Husk look, status/control immunities. Decisions still to make via three-options: the boss start time (e.g. 12:00/12:30/13:00 and enrage/timer rule), the arena design, the move list/phases, the fail condition (time-out defeat) wording and results screen, how it interacts with tiers (HP/damage multipliers x1.5/x2.0, extra moves on harder tiers). Boss design starts when an agent slot is free.
- 2026-10-20 BOSS DIRECTION CLARIFIED (Matt; supersedes the 'time-out defeat' idea in the BOSS DIRECTION row): NO timer on the boss fight: the Elder Treant arrives at the END of the run (once the world has gone light: it is the LAST PUSH) and the fight lasts until you either BEAT it or DIE: no time-out defeat rule. VICTORY BEAT: when the boss dies the WHOLE SCREEN becomes light and filled with life (the dawn flood across the entire view: the Hollow fully restored; colour/life floods the screen, restored ground/props bloom, birds, the AL-6 victory pulse expanded into the final payoff; results screen follows). The boss sheet decides the arrival minute (end of the 15-minute run, at light), the arena, moves/phases, the victory beat design (three options) and how the 15:00 clock behaves (e.g. clock stops/holds while the boss lives; regular waves end or thin).

every gem on the map flies to the hero; the CLOCK HOLDS at 14:00 (moon parked on the dawn line) until the kill; a 3 s arrival beat (ring warning, roots rise, the Treant tears out of the soil, banner 'THE ELDER TREANT WAKES'); (2) ARENA: Root Ring radius 230 built from the Treant's roots, hard wall that does no damage, big enough to kite, camera follows the hero and zooms out ~8% for the fight (portrait own zoom), outside stays grey; one heart per phase change, boss adds drop no hearts; (3) LOOK: THE FIRST HUSK, ELDER (boss-scale grown-bark mask face, amber eyes in sockets, the oldest mask); (4) MOVES: six ground-control moves over three phases (at 66% and 33% HP): slam, root eruption, seed toss (pods -> sapling Husks), vine sweep, thorn fan then ring of thorns, root lanes; each phase entry calls in Husks; every warning escapable for the slowest hero (Elias 76) after a 0.35 s reaction; slam/sweep warnings 1.2 s; boss damage 6/10/15 per phase; HP 6,000 (up from 3,600); bosses immune to control, take Bleed/Burn/Mark; (5) PRESSURE 'the forest leans in': every 30 s the gap between moves shrinks 6% (never below 70% of normal) and the sapling cap rises; warnings never shorter, boss never heals; NO timer; (6) VICTORY radial flood from the boss: 120 ms hit-stop, the Treant kneels and its mask falls off, one green leaf grows and colour runs down the trunk, life floods the whole view and the map (blooms, bird burst, light shafts), the hero says a short line, then the gold dawn results screen; (7) DEFEAT: locked grey beat + dimmed lantern; recap 'Struck down by the Elder Treant's Vine Sweep, 1:21 into the fight' + boss remaining HP line; (8) TIERS (default A, Matt may override): each harder tier adds one rule on top of the locked HP x1.5/x2.0 (tier DAMAGE multipliers apply to the boss: default keeps the locked tiers rule; flagged by the review). DEFAULTS by Claude: Branch-Mane #3 moved from 12:30 to 11:30; one heart per phase change. Targets: keep the 15-30% no-tree win rate, win 60-75% of fights you reach, median fight 75-100 s, boss 20-35% of all defeats (bot-measured in B7); known weak spots: win rate will fall now the kill is required; Rally targets adds not the boss; the fight uses the run-clock HUD/results/dawn effects (re-time AL-6/WL6 arrival 'boss at 10:00 in grey light' to 14:00 at light). BUILD plan B0..B8 per the sheet.

Matt picks all A: (1) endgame gold = cosmetic gold sink once the tree is bought out, Three Dawns excluded from "complete"; (2) "can light now" = ring + rays on every affordable node plus ONE "best next" marker; (3) REOPENS skill-tree-v2 rule C.5: 1000-node layout uses 6 staggered slots per band (today's tree unaffected). | Tree look + connected-unlock rule (2026-10-21) | Look = A Lantern Trails (docs/mockups/tree-screen-v2/): plain sky warming as the tree fills, trails only between lit lanterns plus a spark toward each open one, constellation outline when complete, five states (Lit, Can light, Not enough gold, Challenge teal ring pinnable, Not yet reachable) no padlocks, calm Next-step bar + one best-next pin, ? legend always in toolbar, fading first-visit tip, goals as side-panel/bottom-sheet tab; the faint lines are removed (long threads, hub star, locked threads, guide circles). RULE (Matt): a node is buyable only if directly connected to ANY owned node (branching and several open branches fine) AND its condition is met. Four exceptions accepted: weapon hearts stay linked to the hub as challenges; evolution seals open once their spirit is woken; starter display hearts count as lit; the 9 far group roots each get one gold-only bridge lantern. Guard: every challenge lantern has a gold-only sibling branch, validator refuses a group whose only exits are challenges, timeline sim target 0 runs with nothing to buy. Far next-rank lanterns move beside their parent; 2 "needs all" joins become "any one" (data only, before launch). Build after the economy/balance analysis. | Matt, one-tap |

| BOND SIZE (Matt, 2026-10-24) | The cap-25 Bond bonus was too small to make players want to rotate characters (+4% HP / 3% less damage / +3% speed). New size = MEDIUM + MILESTONE PERKS: about 2.5x: roughly +10% HP, 8% less damage taken, +6% speed, +5% damage at level 25 (spread over 25 levels, still slow to earn), plus a small named perk at Bond levels 5, 10, 15, 20 and 25 (e.g. wider pickup range, ability cooldown, XP gain; same perk ladder for every character so the tree and sim stay character-agnostic, perk flavour/names per character in data). Applied only at run start. Final retune absorbs the extra power (every character gets the same table). Keepsakes/Bond pace unchanged (about 110 typical runs to max, tune after). | Matt, one-tap |
| CHARACTER SYSTEM v2 CH4-CH6 + BOND SIZE BUILT (2026-10-24) | STARTER PICK: a new profile picks 1 hero + 1 spirit (offer = balance.starter {heroes, weapons}, '*' = all, data-driven, validated; chooseStarter grants heart + first evolution gate and saves; the interim auto-grant is now only a safety net). LOADOUT = 3 SEPARATE STEPS: map (FD4 night screen, only once there is a night choice; its button now reads Next) -> character (awake heroes, Bond badge, same body so no pips) -> starting spirit (all weapons; awake ones selectable, sleeping ones dim with their heart's unlock line; kindred mark; 'Walk with <hero> · <spirit>'). CHARACTERS PAGE ('Kindred', title button C; Characters joins the Tree/Almanac/Settings row on wide screens, own row on phones): awake hero = Bond badge + bar, action skill, Lantern/Stride/Ward/Spirits with Bond share, the five named Bond gifts (lit when reached), kindred spirit; sleeping hero = '?', keepsake dots + name + hint progress (no name leak). RESULTS BOND BEAT via the celebration queue (cap 1 big + 3 medium kept): Bond level-up = medium card (+XP, new gift named at milestones) with cue bond_level; keepsake found = medium card (cue keepsake, also an in-run event sound); a hero waking = the big moment (medium if a codex big is already showing). BOND SIZE (Matt's lock): totals at 25 = HP +10%, ward 8%, speed +6%, spirit damage +5% (cycle hp/ward/speed/damage), gifts 5/10/15/20/25 = pickup +15%, ability cooldown -8% (30 s floor), XP +6%, heart heal +15%, gold +10% (generic, same ladder; names per hero in characters.json bondPerks), applied once at run start after the tree's Blessing-cap reference (Bond is its own row outside the tree PE budget: combat 30.99/31, sustain 5.00/5 unchanged). Sanctuary Ring slow 40% -> 20% (decision 1). Fifth Hook now asks for Deep Dusk clears with 2 DIFFERENT heroes (new clearTierHeroes condition; no hero id left in tree data). BIBLE section 3 got the v2 character rules (CLAUDE.md left for Matt: agents do not edit it). Shots docs/screenshots/characters/ld-*, ch-page*, ch-title*, ch-beat* (3 sizes, scripts/ch-ui-shots.mjs: no page scroll, taps >= 44, text >= 12). | Matt (lock), Claude (build) |
